<script data-breeze>
var idx = 1;
slideTop(idx);

function plusSlides(n) {
  slideTop(idx += n);
}

function slideTop(n) {
  var i=0;
  var slideNm = document.querySelectorAll('.topslider');

  if (n > slideNm.length) {idx = 1}
  if (n < 1) {idx = slideNm.length}
  for (i = 0; i < slideNm.length; i++) {
      slideNm[i].style.display = "none";

  }
  slideNm[idx-1].style.display = "block";
}
window.onload= function () {
 setInterval(function(){
     plusSlides(1);
 }, 3000);
 }

</script>

require(['jquery'], function($) {

       $('.block.block-title.search-block').on('click', function() {
        var searchBlockContent = $('.search-block-content');
        
        // Check if the element is already visible (display is inline) or hidden (display is none)
        if (searchBlockContent.css('display') === 'none') {
            // If it is hidden, set display to block
            searchBlockContent.css('display', 'block');
        } else {
            // If it is visible, set display to none
            searchBlockContent.css('display', 'none');
        }
    });

});

<script data-breeze>

    require(['jquery'], ($) => {
        $(document).ready(function(){
            // Get the modal
            var modal = document.getElementById("myModals");

            // Get the <span> element that closes the modal
            var span = document.getElementsByClassName("close-icon")[0];

            // When the user clicks the button, open the modal
            $('#myBtns').on("click", function(e) {
                $('#myModals').show();
            });

            // When the user clicks the button, open the modal
            $('#myBtns').on("click", function(e) {
                $('#myModals').show();
            });
            // When the user clicks the button, close the modal
            $('.close-icon').on("click", function(e) {
                $('#myModals').hide();
            });

            // When the user clicks on <span> (x), close the modal
             if(!typeof span === 'undefined'){
                span.onclick = function() {
                    modal.style.display = "none";
                }
            }

            // When the user clicks anywhere outside of the modal, close it
            window.onclick = function(event) {
              if (event.target == modal) {
                modal.style.display = "none";
              }
            }
        });
      });
</script>

<script data-breeze>

    require(['jquery'], ($) => {
        // Get the modal
        var modal = document.getElementById("modal-pops");

        $('#see-more1').on("click", function(e) {
            $('#modal-pops').show();
        });

        // Get the <span> element that closes the modal
        var span = document.getElementsByClassName("close-icon1")[0];

        // When the user clicks on <span> (x), close the modal

        if(!typeof span === 'undefined'){
            span.onclick = function() {
                modal.style.display = "none";
            }
        }

        // When the user clicks anywhere outside of the modal, close it
        window.onclick = function(event) {
            if (event.target == modal) {
                modal.style.display = "none";
            }
        }
    });
</script>

    <script data-breeze>
        require([
            'jquery'
        ], function ($) {
            $(document).ready(function () {
                $('.input-text.qty').on('change', function() {
                    var checker = $.validator.validateElement($(this));
                    if (!checker) {
                        return false;
                    }
                    var item     = $(this);
                    var prev_qty = $('#val_hidden').val();
                    prev_qty     = parseInt(prev_qty);
                    var qty = Math.abs(parseInt(item.val()));
                    if($.isNumeric(qty)){
                        item.val(qty);
                        if(qty > 10){
                            $('#show_message').html('*Error: Quantity field must be equals or less than 10.');
                            item.val(prev_qty);
                            setTimeout(function(){
                                $('#show_message').html('');
                            }, 5000);
                        }else {
                            if(qty == 0){
                                $('#show_message').html('*Error: Quantity field must be greater than 0.');
                                item.val(prev_qty);
                                setTimeout(function(){
                                    $('#show_message').html('');
                                }, 5000);
                            }else{
                                item.val(qty);
                            }
                        }
                    }else{
                        $('#show_message').html('*Error: Please input number.');
                        item.val(prev_qty);
                        setTimeout(function(){
                            $('#show_message').html('');
                        }, 5000);
                    }
                });
            });
        });
    </script>

<script data-breeze>
    require(['jquery', 'jquery/validate','mage/validation'], function ($) {
    'use strict';
    $(document).ready(function () {

    $('.sizechart-link').insertBefore('.field.qty').attr('style', 'top: 0 !important;');


        function isStoreAvailable(){
            var form = $('#product_addtocart_form');
            const data = form.serialize();
            if(data){
                $.ajax({
                    type: 'POST',
                    url: window.location.origin + '/bookingappointments/customer/getproductstore',
                    data: data,
                    showLoader:true,
                    success: function (data) {
                        if (data.status === 200) {
                            if(data.stores.length > 0){
                                $('#custom-popup-open').show();
                            }else{
                                $('#custom-popup-open').hide();
                            }
                        }
                    }
                })
            }
        }

        if (!$.breeze) {
            setTimeout(isStoreAvailable, 400);
        }else{
            setTimeout(isStoreAvailable, 400);
        }

        $(document).on('click', '.swatch-option', function (e) {
            isStoreAvailable();
        });
        const ajaxStart = () => $('#privilege_loading').show();
        const ajaxDone = () => $('#privilege_loading').hide();
        $('#custom-popup-open').on('click', function (e) {

            e.preventDefault();
            var form = $('#product_addtocart_form');

            if (!form.valid()) {
                return false;
            }

            const data = form.serialize();
            ajaxStart();
            $.ajax({
                type: 'POST',
                url: window.location.origin + '/bookingappointments/customer/getproductstore',
                data: data,
                showLoader:true,
                success: function (data) {
                    if (data.status === 200) {
                        let mediaUrl = window.location.origin+'/media/catalog/product'+data.product.image
                        $('.popup-step.step-1 .productName').text(data.product.name);
                        $('.prodcut-details .productcolor').text(data.product.color);
                        $('.prodcut-details .productsize').text(data.product.size);
                        $('.prodcut-details .productsku').text(data.product.sku);
                        $('.product-booking-info img').attr('src',mediaUrl);
                        $('.product-booking-info img').attr('alt',data.product.name);
                        $('.custom-input.product-details .productName').text(data.product.name);
                        $('.custom-input.product-details .productcolor').text(data.product.color);
                        $('.custom-input.product-details .productsize').text(data.product.size);
                        $('.custom-input.product-details .productsku').text(data.product.sku);
                        $('input[name=product_detail]').val(data.product.sku);
                        $('#custom-popup-modal').fadeIn();
                        $('#popup-step-1').addClass('active');

                        let storeHtml = "";
                        if(data.stores.length > 0){
                            for (var i = 0; i < data.stores.length; i++) {
                                if (parseInt(data.low_stock) < parseInt(data.stores[i].quantity)) {
                                    storeHtml += `
                                    <div class="addres-loc on-store"  data-holiday="${data.stores[i].days_off}" data-code="${data.stores[i].source_code}" data-name="${data.stores[i].name}" data-filter = "${data.stores[i].name}, ${data.stores[i].street}, ${data.stores[i].city}, ${data.stores[i].region}, ${data.stores[i].postcode}">
                                        <h6>${data.stores[i].name}</h6>
                                        <p class="main-address">${data.stores[i].street}, ${data.stores[i].city}, ${data.stores[i].region}, ${data.stores[i].postcode}</p>
                                        <div class="avilability-info">
                                            <p class="avlb-light"><span class="green-light"></span></p>
                                            <p class="stock-avl available">Available</p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                    `;
                                }else{
                                    storeHtml += `
                                    <div class="addres-loc on-store" data-holiday="${data.stores[i].days_off}" data-code="${data.stores[i].source_code}" data-name="${data.stores[i].name}" data-filter = "${data.stores[i].name}, ${data.stores[i].street}, ${data.stores[i].city}, ${data.stores[i].region}, ${data.stores[i].postcode}">
                                        <h6>${data.stores[i].name}</h6>
                                        <p class="main-address">${data.stores[i].street}, ${data.stores[i].city}, ${data.stores[i].region}, ${data.stores[i].postcode}</p>
                                        <div class="avilability-info">
                                            <p class="avlb-light"><span class="red-light"></span></p>
                                            <p class="stock-avl low-stock">Low Stock</p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                    `;
                                }

                            }
                        }else{

                        }
                        $('.main-popup-box.popup-box-2 .address-boxes .addres-loc').remove()
                        $('.main-popup-box.popup-box-2 .address-boxes').append(storeHtml)
                    }else if(data.status === "fail" && data.code == "500" ){
                        $.cookieStorage.set('timingValue', data.otp_timing);
                        $('#custom-popup-modal').fadeIn();
                        $('#popup-step-1').addClass('active');
                        showAlert(data.message);
                    }
                  ajaxDone()
                }
            })
        });

         var customPopup = $('#custom-popup-open');
         $('#product-addtocart-button').after(customPopup);
         $('#booking-form').validation();

        $('.custom-popup-close, .custom-popup-overlay').on('click', function () {
            $('#custom-popup-modal').fadeOut();
            resetSteps();
        });

        $('.back-button').on('click', function () {
            $('#popup-step-2').removeClass('active').fadeOut();
            $('#popup-step-1').addClass('active').fadeIn();
        });

        $('.cancel').on('click', function () {
            $('#custom-popup-modal').fadeOut();
            resetSteps();
        });

        function resetSteps() {
            $('#popup-step-1').addClass('active').fadeIn();
            $('#popup-step-2').removeClass('active').hide();
        }
    });
});
</script>

<script data-breeze>
require(['jquery'], function ($) {
    $(document).ready(function () {
        if ($('body').hasClass('catalog-product-view')) {
            setTimeout(function () {
                $('.amasty-label-container').each(function () {
                    var $label = $(this);

                    // Check if the label is inside .breeze-gallery within .product.media
                    if ($label.closest('.product.media').find('.breeze-gallery.vertical').length) {
                        var $mainImageWrapper = $label.closest('.product.media')
                            .find('.breeze-gallery.vertical .main-image-wrapper')
                            .first();

                        if ($mainImageWrapper.length) {
                            $label.appendTo($mainImageWrapper);
                        }
                    }
                });
            }, 1000); // 1 second delay
        }
    });
});
</script>

            setTimeout(function () {
                $('.amasty-label-container').each(function () {
                    var $label = $(this);

                    // Check if the label is inside .breeze-gallery within .product.media
                    if ($label.closest('.product.media').find('.breeze-gallery.horizontal').length) {

<script data-breeze>

require(['jquery','{{brand_name}}_Customer/js/customer-data'], 
    function ($,customerData) {

    const currentWwebsiteId = 10;
    var products;
    var productName;
    var countFirstLoad = 4;
    var websiteId = window.checkout.websiteId;
    
    $(document).ready(function() {

        try {
            window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
            var productIds = [];
            var productId = $('.price-box.price-final_price').attr('data-product-id');
            productIds.push(productId);

            $.ajax({
                type: 'POST',
                url: window.location.origin + '/datalayer/ga/cartlayer',
                data: {'ids': productIds , 'requestFrom' : 'recent_view' },
                success: function (data) {

                    let _productName = data[0].product_name ? data[0].product_name : "Not Defined";
                    let _sku = data[0].sku ? data[0].sku : "Not Defined";
                    let _productPrice = data[0].price ? data[0].price : "Not Defined";
                    let _brand = data[0].origin_brand ? data[0].origin_brand : "Not Defined";
                    let _category = data[0].category ? data[0].category : "Not Defined";
                    let _category1 = data[0].category1 ? data[0].category1 : "Not Defined";
                    let _category2 = data[0].category2 ? data[0].category2 : "Not Defined";
                    let _category3 = data[0].category3 ? data[0].category3 : "Not Defined";

                    var products = [{
                        'item_name': _productName,
                        'item_id': _sku,
                        'price': _productPrice,
                        'item_brand': _brand,
                        'item_category': _category,
                        'item_category2': _category1,
                        'item_category3': _category2,
                        'item_category4': _category3,
                        'item_variant': "Not Defined",
                        'quantity': 1
                    }];

                    dataLayer.push({
                        'event': 'view_item',
                        'eventCategory':'ecommerce',
                        'eventAction':'product_detail',
                        'eventLabel':_productName,
                        'ecommerce': {
                            'items': products
                        }

                    });
                    
                }
            });
        } catch (err) {
            console.log('error product view : ' + err.message);
        }
    });

    $('#product-addtocart-button').click(function(e)
    {
        e.stopPropagation();
        try {
            var cart = customerData.get('cart');
            var productName = $('.page-title-wrapper span').text(); 
            var productSku  = $('#product_addtocart_form').attr('data-product-sku');
            var productQty  = $('#qty').val();
            var productCategory = "Coach ";

            generateOptionProduct(cart, productName, productSku, productCategory);
            
        } catch (err) {
            console.log('error ga add to cart : ' + err.message);
        } 
    });

    $('.input-text.qty').on('change', function() {

        try {
            var cart = customerData.get('cart');
            var count = cart().summary_count;

            productName = $('.page-title-wrapper span').text(); 
            var productSku  = $('.product-info-stock-sku .product.attribute.sku .value').text();
            var productQty  = $(this).val();
            var productPrice = $('.product-info-main .price-final_price').find('.price-wrapper ').attr('data-price-amount');
            var productBrand = "Coach";
            var productCategory = "Coach ";

            var itemCart = [{
                'name': productName,
                'id': productSku,
                'price': productPrice,
                'brand': productBrand,
                'category': productCategory,
                'quantity': productQty
            }];

            window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
            dataLayer.push({
                'event': 'addToCart',
                'eventCategory':'Ecommerce',
                'eventAction':'Add To Cart',
                'eventLabel':productName,
                'ecommerce': {
                    'items': itemCart
                }
            });

        } catch (err) {
            console.log('error ga change quantity : ' + err.message);
        }
        
    });

    
    var isDesktopRecentTrigger = false;
    var productList = $.storage.ns('product_data_storage').get();

    window.addEventListener('scroll', function(e) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        e.preventDefault();

        try {
            if( $('.recent.products-grid').length > 0 )
            { 
                if (elementInView($('.recent.products-grid')) && isDesktopRecentTrigger == false) 
                {
                    var itemsRecent = [];
                    var products = [];
                    var productIds = [];
                    var productVariant = 'Not Defined';

                    let index = 1;
                    Object.keys(productList).forEach(function(key){
                        if (index > countFirstLoad) return;
                        productIds.push(key);
                        index++;
                    });

                    $.ajax({
                        type: 'POST',
                        url: window.location.origin + '/datalayer/ga/cartlayer',
                        data: {'ids': productIds , 'requestFrom' : 'recent_view' },
                        success: function (data) {

                            let idxData = 1;
                            $.each(data, function(index, itemProd){

                                let _productName = itemProd.product_name ? itemProd.product_name : "Not Defined";
                                let _sku = itemProd.sku ? itemProd.sku : "Not Defined";
                                let _productPrice = itemProd.price ? itemProd.price : "Not Defined";
                                let _brand = itemProd.origin_brand ? itemProd.origin_brand : "Not Defined";
                                let _category = itemProd.category ? itemProd.category : "Not Defined";
                                let _category1 = itemProd.category1 ? itemProd.category1 : "Not Defined";
                                let _category2 = itemProd.category2 ? itemProd.category2 : "Not Defined";
                                let _category3 = itemProd.category3 ? itemProd.category3 : "Not Defined";

                                var products = {
                                    'item_name': _productName,
                                    'item_id': _sku,
                                    'price': _productPrice,
                                    'item_brand': _brand,
                                    'item_category': _category,
                                    'item_category2': _category1,
                                    'item_category3': _category2,
                                    'item_category4': _category3,
                                    'item_variant': productVariant,
                                    'quantity': 1,
                                    'index' : idxData
                                };

                                itemsRecent.push(products);
                                idxData++;
                            });


                            window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
                            dataLayer.push({
                                'event': 'view_item_list',
                                'eventCategory':'ecommerce',
                                'eventAction': 'product_view',
                                'eventLabel': 'Recently Viewed',
                                'ecommerce': {                              
                                    'items': itemsRecent
                                }
                            });	
                            
                        }
                    });

                    isDesktopRecentTrigger = true;
                }
            }
        } catch (err) {
            console.log('error recent view ga : ' + err.message);
        }

    });
                
    $(document).on('click', '.recent.products-grid li a', function() {
        try {
            var linkProduct = $(this).attr('href');
            var productIds = [];
            var _index = $(this).parents('.product-item').index() + 1;

            Object.keys(productList).forEach(function(key){
                if (productList[key].url = linkProduct) {
                    productIds.push(key);
                }
            })
            
            sendRecentViewClick(productIds, _index)
        } catch (err) {
            console.log('error click recent view : ' + err.message);
        }

    });

    $(document).on('click', '.recent.products-grid li img', function() {
        try {
            var parent = $(this).parents('.product-item');
            var linkProduct = parent.find('a.product-item-link').attr('href');
            var productIds = [];
            var _index = parent.index() + 1;

            Object.keys(productList).forEach(function(key){
                if (productList[key].url = linkProduct) {
                    productIds.push(key);
                }
            })
            
            sendRecentViewClick(productIds, _index)
        } catch (err) {
            console.log('error click recent view : ' + err.message);
        }

    });

    function elementInView(elem){
        return ($(window).height() + $(window).scrollTop()) > $(elem).offset().top;
    };

    function sendRecentViewClick(productIds, _index)
    {
        try {
            $.ajax({
                type: 'POST',
                url: window.location.origin + '/datalayer/ga/cartlayer',
                data: {'ids': productIds , 'requestFrom' : 'recent_view' },
                success: function (data) {
                    let _productName = data[0].product_name ? data[0].product_name : "Not Defined";
                    let _sku = data[0].sku ? data[0].sku : "Not Defined";
                    let _productPrice = data[0].price ? data[0].price : "Not Defined";
                    let _brand = data[0].origin_brand ? data[0].origin_brand : "Not Defined";
                    let _category = data[0].category ? data[0].category : "Not Defined";
                    let _category1 = data[0].category1 ? data[0].category1 : "Not Defined";
                    let _category2 = data[0].category2 ? data[0].category2 : "Not Defined";
                    let _category3 = data[0].category3 ? data[0].category3 : "Not Defined";

                    var products = [{
                        'item_name': _productName,
                        'item_id': _sku,
                        'price': _productPrice,
                        'item_brand': _brand,
                        'item_category': _category,
                        'item_category2': _category1,
                        'item_category3': _category2,
                        'item_category4': _category3,
                        'item_variant': "Not Defined",
                        'index' : _index,
                        'quantity': 1
                    }];

                    if (typeof productCart !== 'undefined') {
                        productCart.push(products);
                    }

                    window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
                    dataLayer.push({
                        'event': 'select_item',
                        'eventCategory':'ecommerce',
                        'eventAction': 'product_click',
                        'eventLabel':_productName,
                        'ecommerce': {                              
                            'items': products
                        }
                    });           
                    
                }
            });
        } catch (err) {
            console.log('error click recent view : ' + err.message);
        }
    }

    function generateOptionProduct(cart, productName, productSku, productCategory) {

        try {
            var productPrice = $('.price-wrapper').attr('data-price-amount');
            var productVariant = '';
   
            if ($('.swatch-attribute.color .swatch-attribute-label').length > 0) {
                productVariant += $('.swatch-attribute.color .swatch-attribute-label').text().trim();
            }
            if ($('.swatch-attribute.color .swatch-attribute-selected-option').length > 0) {
                productVariant += $('.swatch-attribute.color .swatch-attribute-selected-option').text().trim() + ' | ';
            }
            if ($('.swatch-attribute.size .swatch-attribute-label').length > 0) {
                productVariant += $('.swatch-attribute.size .swatch-attribute-label').text().trim();
            }
            if ($('.swatch-attribute.size .swatch-attribute-selected-option').length > 0) {
                productVariant += $('.swatch-attribute.size .swatch-attribute-selected-option').text().trim();
            }

            if (productVariant == '') {
                productVariant = 'Not Defined';
            }

            $.ajax({
                type: 'POST',
                url: window.location.origin + '/datalayer/ga/cartlayer',
                data: {'product_sku': productSku , 'typeProduct' : 'sku' },
                success: function (data) {
                    console.log('data',data)
                    let _productName = data.product_name ? data.product_name : "Not Defined";
                    let _sku = data.sku ? data.sku : "Not Defined";
                    let _productPrice = data.price ? data.price : "Not Defined";
                    let _brand = data.origin_brand ? data.origin_brand : "Not Defined";
                    let _category = data.category ? data.category : "Not Defined";
                    let _category1 = data.category1 ? data.category1 : "Not Defined";
                    let _category2 = data.category2 ? data.category2 : "Not Defined";
                    let _category3 = data.category3 ? data.category3 : "Not Defined";

                    var products = [{
                        item_name: _productName,
                        item_id: productSku,
                        price: productPrice,
                        item_brand: _brand,
                        item_category: _category,
                        item_category2: 'Not Defined',
                        item_category3: 'Not Defined',
                        item_category4: 'Not Defined',
                        item_variant: productVariant,
                        quantity: String($('#qty').val())
                    }];

                    if (typeof productCart !== 'undefined') {
                        productCart.push(products);
                    }

                    window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
                    dataLayer.push({
                        'event': 'add_to_cart',
                        'eventCategory':'Ecommerce',
                        'eventAction':'add_to_cart',
                        'eventLabel':productName,
                        'ecommerce': {                              
                            'items': products
                        }
                    });

                    let is_part_of_bundle = false;

                    if ($('body').hasClass('page-product-bundle')) {
                        _productPrice = $('.price-container.price-configured_price > span[data-price-type="finalPrice"] span').text() ? $('.price-container.price-configured_price > span[data-price-type="finalPrice"] span').text() : 0;
                        _productPrice = _productPrice.replace("Rp ", "");
                        _productPrice = _productPrice.replace(".", "");
                        _productPrice = _productPrice.replace(".", "");
                        is_part_of_bundle = true;
                    }

    <script data-breeze>
        require(['jquery'], ($) => {
            $(function () {
                // window.onscroll = function () {
                //     if (document.body.scrollTop > 400 || document.documentElement.scrollTop > 400) {
                //         $('#back-to-top').css('display', 'block');
                //     } else {
                //         $('#back-to-top').css('display', 'none');
                //     }
                // };
                $('#back-to-top').on('click', function () {
                    document.body.scrollTop = 0;
                    document.documentElement.scrollTop = 0;
                });
            });

            if ($('body').hasClass('catalog-category-view') || $('body').hasClass('catalog-product-view')) {
                $('.mmn.mobile').find('.accordion-mob').on('click', function (e) {
                    console.log('mobnav clicked');
                    $('.accordion-mob').not(this).addClass('d-collapsed');
                    var isActive = $(this).hasClass("show");
                    $('.accordion-mob').removeClass('show')
                    if (!isActive) {
                        $(this).removeClass('d-collapsed');
                        $(this).addClass('show');
                    }
                    else if (isActive) {
                        $(this).removeClass('show');
                        $(this).toggleClass("d-collapsed");
                    }
                    $(this).next().toggle('fast');
                    //Hide the other panels
                    $(".d-collapse").not($(this).next()).toggle('fast');
                    e.preventDefault();
                });
            }

        });
    </script>

    // toggle collapse of specified content
    function toggleContent(content) {
        if (content.style.maxHeight) {
            content.style.maxHeight = null;
        } else {
            content.style.maxHeight = content.scrollHeight + 'px';

}

    -ms-transform: scale(-1, 1);
    transform: scale(-1, 1)
}

.fa-flip-vertical {

    -webkit-transform: scale(1, -1);

    transform: scale(1, -1)
}

@-webkit-keyframes bounce {
    0%,20%,53%,80%,to {
        -webkit-animation-timing-function: cubic-bezier(.215,.61,.355,1);
        animation-timing-function: cubic-bezier(.215,.61,.355,1);
        -webkit-transform: translateZ(0);
        transform: translateZ(0)
    }

    40%,43% {
        -webkit-transform: translate3d(0,-30px,0);
        transform: translate3d(0,-30px,0)
    }

    40%,43%,70% {
        -webkit-animation-timing-function: cubic-bezier(.755,.05,.855,.06);
        animation-timing-function: cubic-bezier(.755,.05,.855,.06)
    }

    70% {
        -webkit-transform: translate3d(0,-15px,0);
        transform: translate3d(0,-15px,0)
    }

    90% {
        -webkit-transform: translate3d(0,-4px,0);
        transform: translate3d(0,-4px,0)
    }
}

@keyframes bounce {

<script data-breeze>
    require([
        'jquery'
    ],function($) {
         $(document).ready(function() {
            if ($('body').hasClass('catalog-product-view')) {
$(".ves-megamenu .dropdown-menu ul.content_mnav  a").on('click', function(){
var _url = $(this).attr('href');
window.location.href = window.location.origin + _url;
});
                $('.mobile_content').on('click', function (e) {
                    $(this).find('.accordion-mob').addClass('d-collapsed');
                    var isActive = $(this).find('.accordion-mob').hasClass("show");
                    $(this).find('.accordion-mob').removeClass('show')
                    if (!isActive) {
                        $(this).find('.accordion-mob').removeClass('d-collapsed');
                        $(this).find('.accordion-mob').addClass('show');
                    }
                    else if (isActive) {
                        $(this).find('.accordion-mob').removeClass('show');
                        $(this).find('.accordion-mob').toggleClass("d-collapsed");
                    }
                    $(this).find('.accordion-mob').next().toggle('fast');
                    //Hide the other panels
                    $(".d-collapse").not($(this).find('.accordion-mob').next()).toggle('fast');
                    e.preventDefault();
                });
            }
         });  
     });
</script>
